# בנק שאלות: נוסחים מסקרים קיימים (טיוטה)

> **מטרה (נוה, 2026-10-27):** להעתיק את כל השאלות מסקרים אחרים. נערוך ונשנה אותן בהמשך.
> **מעמד:** חומר גלם פנימי. **אסור לפרסם למשתמשים** לפני תשובה לשאלה D4 לעורך הדין (זכויות יוצרים על נוסחים), ב-`docs/legal/lawyer-questions.md`.
> **שיטה:** הטקסט חולץ אוטומטית מקובצי ה-PDF של הנתונים, עם `pdftotext`. ייתכנו שגיאות חילוץ: שורות שנחתכו או אפשרויות חסרות. **דורש אימות:** כל שאלה מול ה-PDF לפני שמשתמשים בה.
> **היקף נוכחי:** מדד הקול הישראלי (IDI), כ-80 קובצי נתונים מאוגוסט 2019 עד ספטמבר 2026, ומדד הדמוקרטיה הישראלית (IDI, שנתי) לשנים 2022–2025, מנספח 1 ("Questionnaire and Distribution of Responses"). מקורות נוספים יתווספו בהמשך.
> **ידוע:** מדד הדמוקרטיה 2023 חולץ מחדש בשיטה ייעודית (63 מתוך 64 שאלות). בחלק מהשאלות האפשרויות חלקיות. **הכול חולץ אוטומטית, וכל שאלה דורשת בדיקה מול המקור.**

סה"כ 1313 שאלות: 203 במהדורות 2025–2026, 821 במהדורות ישנות של מדד הקול, ו-289 ממדד הדמוקרטיה.

מפתח מזהה: `IVI-<קובץ>-<מספר שאלה>` למדד הקול, ו-`IDI-democracy-index-<שנה>-<מספר שאלה>` למדד הדמוקרטיה. השאלות מובאות בשפת המקור (אנגלית), בלי תרגום.

## israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data

מקור: [israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data.pdf](https://en.idi.org.il/media/28945/israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data.pdf) · [מאמר](https://en.idi.org.il/articles/61601)

- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-1:** How do you feel about Israel’s situation in the following areas in the foreseeable future? Democratic rule
  - אפשרויות: Very optimistic / Quite optimistic / Quite pessimistic / Very pessimistic / Don’t know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-2:** How do you feel about Israel’s situation in the following areas in the foreseeable future? The security situation
  - אפשרויות: Very optimistic / Quite optimistic / Quite pessimistic / Very pessimistic / Don’t know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-3:** How do you feel about Israel’s situation in the following areas in the foreseeable future? The economic situation
  - אפשרויות: Very optimistic / Quite optimistic / Quite pessimistic / Very pessimistic / Don’t know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-4:** How do you feel about Israel’s situation in the following areas in the foreseeable future? Social cohesion
  - אפשרויות: Very optimistic / Quite optimistic / Quite pessimistic / Very pessimistic / Don’t know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-5:** Recently, the security cabinet approved the expansion of military operations in Gaza, including taking and holding territory. Do you support or oppose this decision?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly support it / Somewhat support it / Somewhat oppose it / Strongly oppose it / Don’t know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-6:** In your estimation, for how much longer will Israeli society be able to bear the burden of continued fighting in Gaza and the confrontation in the north?
  - אפשרויות: A few months / Six months to a year / A year or more / Don't know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-7:** According to media reports, the release of all the Israeli hostages may be a possibility as part of a deal that includes the cessation of hostilities and the complete withdrawal of the IDF from the Gaza Strip. In your opinion, should Israel agree to a deal on this basis?
  - אפשרויות: Certain it should / Think it should / Think it should not / Certain it should not / Don't know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-8:** Do you support or oppose establishing Jewish settlements in the Gaza Strip?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly support / Somewhat support / Somewhat oppose / Strongly oppose / Don’t know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-9:** In your opinion, who should control the Gaza Strip after the end of the war? [Responses presented in a random order]
  - אפשרויות: Israel / A multinational force / The Palestinian Authority / A weakened Hamas / Egypt / Other / Don't know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-10:** In your opinion, given the current circumstances, is Israel’s leadership doing its utmost to secure the release of the hostages?
  - אפשרויות: Certain it is / Think it is / Think it is not / Certain it is not / Don’t know
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-august-2025-eng-data-11:** Five years ago, Israel signed the Abraham Accords with the United Arab Emirates and Bahrain. Since then, in your opinion, have these agreements met the expectations for them?
  - אפשרויות: Certain they have / Think they have / Think they have not / Certain they have not / Don’t know

## מדד הקול הישראלי (ישן): israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng

מקור: [israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng.pdf](https://en.idi.org.il/media/13238/israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng.pdf) · [מאמר](https://en.idi.org.il/articles/28560)

- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-1:** How do you feel about the state of democratic governance in Israel in the foreseeable future?
  - אפשרויות: Very optimistic / Moderately optimistic / Moderately pessimistic / Very pessimistic / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-2:** And how do you feel about Israel’s security situation in the foreseeable future?
  - אפשרויות: Very optimistic / Moderately optimistic / Moderately pessimistic / Very pessimistic / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-3:** In about another month Knesset elections will be held. Compared to the previous elections in April, are you following the current election campaign to the same extent, less, or more?
  - אפשרויות: Following the current election campaign / Following the current election campaign a / Following the current election campaign to / Following the current election campaign a / Following the current election campaign / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-4:** Some claim that Israel’s decision to bar the entry of the two American congresswomen from the Democratic Party was unwise because it will seriously damage Israel’s relations with that party, for which most American Jews vote. Others claim that barring their entry was the right decision because these congresswomen have antisemitic views and support a boycott of Israel, so that their visit would have caused Israel great harm. With which claim do you agree more?
  - אפשרויות: Agree more with the first claim, that barring / Agree more with the second claim, that / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-5:** If the Trump administration announces support for Netanyahu’s declarations about annexing Area C (the area of the West Bank/Judea and Samaria that is under Israel’s full control), will you support or oppose such an annexation?
  - אפשרויות: I’m sure I will support it / I think I will support it / I think I will oppose it / I’m sure I will oppose it / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-6:** According to estimates, there are now about 200,000 Palestinians living in Area C. If Israel indeed annexes this land, what, in your opinion, should be done regarding these Palestinians:
  - אפשרויות: Transfer them to parts of the West Bank that / Allow those Palestinians who want to / Allow those Palestinians who want to / Grant the Palestinians who want to remain / Other / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-7:** If Israel annexes Area C, will you support or oppose establishing a Palestinian state in the rest of the West Bank/Judea and Samaria?
  - אפשרויות: I’m sure I will support it / I think I will support it / I think I will oppose it / I’m sure I will oppose it / Other / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-8:** To what extent do you or do you not have trust in the propriety of the Knesset elections, that is, that the results to be announced will accurately reflect how the public voted?
  - אפשרויות: I have complete trust in the propriety of the / I have quite a lot of trust in the propriety of / I have quite little trust in the propriety of the / I have no trust in the propriety of the / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-9:** [Only for those who answered that they do not have trust in the propriety of the elections, answers 3 and 4 in the previous question] What, in your opinion, could impair the propriety of the elections? [More than one answer can be marked]
  - אפשרויות: Double or forged voting, for example, with / Skewed counting of voting slips by the local / Skewed tallying of the overall results in the / Involvement in the electoral process by / Threats against the voters by activists of / Other / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-10:** Which government would you want to be formed after the elections?
  - אפשרויות: A right-wing government headed by / A unity government headed by Netanyahu / A unity government headed by Gantz / A center-left government headed by Gantz / None of them / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-11:** On a scale of 1 to 5, with 1 referring to left and 5 referring to right, where would you locate Avigdor Lieberman, head of the Yisrael Beiteinu Party?
  - אפשרויות: 1 – Left / 3 – Center / 5 – Right / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1908_eng-12:** Avigdor Lieberman states repeatedly that his party will join only a unity government that is based on the Likud and Blue and White parties. Do you believe him or not believe him?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly believe him / Moderately believe him / Don’t believe him so much / Don’t believe him at all / Don't know/Refuse to answer

- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1909_eng-1:** How do you feel about the state of democratic governance in Israel in the foreseeable future?

  - אפשרויות: Very optimistic / Moderately optimistic / Moderately pessimistic / Very pessimistic / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1909_eng-3:** Are you satisfied or dissatisfied with the results of the latest elections ?
  - אפשרויות: Very satisfied / Moderately satisfied / Not so satisfied / Not satisfied at all / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1909_eng-4:** To what extent do you trust or not trust the propriety of the Knesset elections, meaning that the results that were announced accurately reflect how the public voted?
  - אפשרויות: I have full trust in the propriety of the / I have a lot of trust in the propriety of the / I have quite little trust in the propriety of the / I have no trust in the propriety of the / Don't know/Refuse to answer

  - אפשרויות: The establishment of a national unity / Going to new elections / None of the above / Don't know/Refuse to answer

  - אפשרויות: Strongly oppose / Moderately oppose / Moderately support / Strongly support / Don't know/Refuse to answer

Do you believe him or not believe him when he says he will not join any other government?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly believe him / Moderately believe him / Don’t believe him so much / Don’t believe him at all / Don't know/Refuse to answer

  - אפשרויות: Very high chances / Moderately high chances / Moderately low chances / Very low chances / Don't know/Refuse to answer

Here too Netanyahu would agree to retire from public life, but he would not have to admit guilt. Do you support or oppose this proposal for a pardon?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly support / Moderately support / Moderately oppose / Strongly oppose / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1909_eng-11:** In general, from Israel’s standpoint, how, in your view, was the year that will soon end?
  - אפשרויות: Very good / Moderately good / Moderately bad / Very bad / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1909_eng-12:** And in general, how, in your view, will the New Year be from the standpoint of the country?
  - אפשרויות: Much better than the year that is / A little better than the year that is / Like the year that is ending / Much worse than the year that is / A little worse than the year that is / Don't know/Refuse to answer

- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1912_eng-8:** In your assessment, would a unification of the right-wing parties (Jewish Home, National Union, and Otzma Yehudit) strengthen or weaken the right- wing bloc in the upcoming Knesset elections?
  - אפשרויות: Strengthen / Neither strengthen nor weaken / Weaken / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1912_eng-9:** And would a unification of the left-wing parties (Labor-Gesher, Meretz, and Democratic Israel) strengthen or weaken the center-left bloc in the upcoming Knesset elections?
  - אפשרויות: Strengthen / Neither strengthen nor weaken / Weaken / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1912_eng-10:** Prime Minister Netanyahu claims that his indictment was the result only of personal persecution because he is a Prime Minister from the right-wing. Do you agree or oppose this statement?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly agree / Moderately agree / Moderately oppose / Strongly oppose
- **IVI-israeli_voice_index_data_1912_eng-11:** With the end of the civil year, please give a grade to the functioning of the state over the past year in each of the following areas: In the economic area
  - אפשרויות: 1 - Poor / 5 - Excellent / Don't know/Refuse to answer / 1 - Poor

- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-2:** And how do you feel about Israel’s security situation in the foreseeable future?
  - אפשרויות: Very optimistic / Moderately optimistic / Moderately pessimistic / Very pessimistic / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-3:** In about another month and a half, elections will be held for the Knesset. Compared to the campaign for the previous elections in September, are you following the current campaign to the same extent, less, or more?
  - אפשרויות: Following the current campaign more / Following the current campaign less / Following it to the same extent / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-4:** Do you agree or disagree with the claim that Election Day should count as a vacation day only for those who have proved that they voted?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly agree / Moderately agree / Don’t agree so much / Don’t agree at all / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-5:** There are countries - Belgium, for example - in which those who do not vote in the elections pay a fine. Would you support or oppose making it so that those in Israel who do not vote would also have to pay a fine?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly oppose / Moderately oppose / Moderately support / Strongly support / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-6:** What, in your opinion, is the main issue that will affect how Israelis vote in the upcoming Knesset elections? (present in rotation)
  - אפשרויות: The cost of living and of housing / The security situation / The Netanyahu investigations / Religion and state issues / Jewish-Arab relations in the country / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-7:** If the Knesset elections were to be held today, which party would you vote for?
  - אפשרויות: Labor-Gesher-Meretz / Likud / Blue and White / Yisrael Beiteinu / Shas / Yamina / Torah Judaism / Otzma Yehudit / Joint List (Hadash, Ta’al, Ra’am, Balad) / Another party
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-8:** To what extent are you satisfied or dissatisfied with the list of candidates for the Knesset of the party you are planning to vote for?
  - אפשרויות: Very satisfied / Moderately satisfied / Not so satisfied / Not satisfied at all / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-9:** To what extent do you have or not have trust in the propriety of the Knesset elections, that is, that the results to be announced will accurately reflect the voting of the public?
  - אפשרויות: I have full trust in the propriety of the elections / I have quite a lot of trust in the propriety of the / I have quite little trust in the propriety of the / I have no trust at all in the propriety of the elections / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-10:** Do you agree or disagree with the claim that to ensure, this time, a decisive result in the upcoming elections, one should vote for one of the large parties and not for a small party, even if its positions better accord with those of the voter?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly agree / Moderately agree / Don’t agree so much / Don’t agree at all / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-11:** The peace plan that President Trump will soon present will apparently include recognition of a Palestinian state. In your opinion, should Israel agree to any plan that includes such recognition?
  - אפשרויות: I’m sure Israel should agree / I think Israel should agree / I think Israel should not agree / I’m sure Israel should not agree / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-12:** Some claim that presenting the peace plan at the current time adds up to U.S. intervention in the Israeli election campaign, and that its purpose is to help Netanyahu win. Do you agree or disagree with this claim?
  - אפשרויות: Strongly agree / Moderately agree / Don’t agree so much / Don’t agree at all / Don't know/Refuse to answer
- **IVI-israeli-voice-index-data-13:** If diplomatic negotiations on Trump’s peace plan were soon to begin, who, in your opinion, would conduct them better on Israel’s behalf, Netanyahu or Gantz?
  - אפשרויות: Netanyahu would conduct them better / Gantz would conduct them better / The two of them would conduct them / Don't know/Refuse to answer

  - אפשרויות: Appropriate / Too severe / Too mild / Don’t know/Not relevant to me

## מדד הדמוקרטיה הישראלית: democracy-index-2023

מקור: [the-israeli-democracy-index-2023-english.pdf](https://en.idi.org.il/media/24389/the-israeli-democracy-index-2023-english.pdf) · נספח 1, עמ' 179–223

> חולץ מחדש ב-2026-10-27 בשיטה ייעודית: `pdftotext -layout` עם ניקוי של שוליים ("Discussion on p.") וזיהוי של שאלות-אם. שאלה 25 לא זוהתה. בחלק מהשאלות האפשרויות קטועות, למשל "Strongly" במקום "Strongly agree". **חולץ אוטומטית, דורש בדיקה מול המקור.**

- **IDI-democracy-index-2023-1:** How would you characterize Israel’s overall situation today?
  - אפשרויות: Very / Good / So-so / Bad / Very bad / Don’t
- **IDI-democracy-index-2023-2:** To what extent do you feel part of the State of Israel and its problems?
  - אפשרויות: Very much / Quite a lot / Not so / Not at all / Don’t know
- **IDI-democracy-index-2023-3:** How would you rate the level of solidarity (sense of “togetherness”) of Israeli society as a whole (Jews, Arabs, and all other citizens), where 1 = no solidarity at all and 10 = very high level of solidarity?
  - אפשרויות: 1 – No / 10 – Very / Don't Total Mean
- **IDI-democracy-index-2023-4:** (Jewish respondents) How would you rate the level of solidarity (sense of “togetherness”) of Jewish society in Israel?
  - אפשרויות: 1 – No / 10 – Very / Don't Total Mean
- **IDI-democracy-index-2023-5:** How would you rate Israeli democracy today on a scale of 1 to 5, where 1 = very poor and 5 = excellent?
